#include "StorageManager.h"

// Storage callback instance
Storage::StorageCallback Storage::_callback = nullptr;

bool Storage::init() {
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][init] Initializing storage system...");
    #endif
    #endif

    // Mount LittleFS
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 3
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][DEBUG] Attempting to mount LittleFS...");
    #endif
    #endif

    if (!LittleFS.begin()) {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][ERROR] Failed to mount LittleFS");
        #endif
        #endif
        triggerCallback("mount_failed", "LittleFS mount failed");
        return false;
    }

    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][INFO] LittleFS mounted successfully");
    #endif
    #endif

    // Initialize standard files
    if (initializeStandardFiles()) {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][INFO] Standard files initialized successfully");
        #endif
        #endif
    } else {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][WARNING] Some standard files may not have been initialized");
        #endif
        #endif
    }

    // Display file system info
    getFSInfo();

    // List root directory
    listDir("/");

    triggerCallback("mounted", "LittleFS mounted successfully");
    return true;
}

bool Storage::exists(const String& path) {
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][exists] Checking if file exists: " + path);
    #endif
    #endif
    return LittleFS.exists(path);
}

bool Storage::remove(const String& path) {
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][remove] Attempting to remove file: " + path);
    #endif
    #endif

    if (!LittleFS.exists(path)) {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][ERROR] File not found for removal: " + path);
        #endif
        #endif
        triggerCallback("remove_failed", "File not found: " + path);
        return false;
    }

    bool success = LittleFS.remove(path);
    if (success) {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][remove] File removed successfully: " + path);
        #endif
        #endif
        triggerCallback("removed", path);
    } else {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][ERROR] Failed to remove file: " + path);
        #endif
        #endif
        triggerCallback("remove_failed", "Failed to remove: " + path);
    }
    return success;
}

bool Storage::mkdir(const String& path) {
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][mkdir] Attempting to create directory: " + path);
    #endif
    #endif

    bool success = LittleFS.mkdir(path);
    if (success) {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][mkdir] Directory created successfully: " + path);
        #endif
        #endif
        triggerCallback("directory_created", path);
    } else {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][mkdir] Directory may already exist: " + path);
        #endif
        #endif
    }
    return success;
}

String Storage::read(const String& path) {
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][read] Attempting to read file: " + path);
    #endif
    #endif

    File file = LittleFS.open(path, "r");
    if (!file) {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][read] Failed to open file for reading: " + path);
        #endif
        #endif
        triggerCallback("read_failed", "Failed to open for reading: " + path);
        return "";
    }

    String data = file.readString();
    file.close();

    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 3
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][readString] Read " + String(data.length()) + " bytes from " + path);
    #endif
    #endif

    triggerCallback("read", path + " (" + String(data.length()) + " bytes)");
    return data;
}

bool Storage::write(const String& path, const String& data) {
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][write] Attempting to write to file: " + path);
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][write] Data size: " + String(data.length()) + " bytes");
    #endif
    #endif

    File file = LittleFS.open(path, "w");
    if (!file) {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][open] Failed to open file for writing: " + path);
        #endif
        #endif
        triggerCallback("write_failed", "Failed to open for writing: " + path);
        return false;
    }

    size_t bytesWritten = file.print(data);
    file.close();

    if (bytesWritten > 0) {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][open] Successfully wrote " + String(bytesWritten) + " bytes to " + path);
        #endif
        #endif
        triggerCallback("written", path + " (" + String(bytesWritten) + " bytes)");
        return true;
    } else {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][open] Failed to write data to file: " + path);
        #endif
        #endif
        triggerCallback("write_failed", "Failed to write data to: " + path);
        return false;
    }
}

    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL

    }

    File file = root.openNextFile();
    while (file) {
    // no subdirectories are supported
    if (!file.isDirectory()) {
        String fileInfo = String(file.name()) + " (" + String(file.size()) + " bytes)";
        
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
            #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
                Debug::println("  " + fileInfo);

        }

    triggerCallback("listed", path);
}

bool Storage::initializeStandardFiles() {
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][initializeStandardFiles] Initializing standard configuration files...");
    #endif
    #endif

    bool success = true;

    // Create config directory if it doesn't exist
    if (!exists("/config")) {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][initializeStandardFiles] Creating config directory...");
        #endif
        #endif
        if (!mkdir("/config")) {
            #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
            #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
                Debug::println("[STORAGE][initializeStandardFiles] Failed to create config directory");
            #endif
            #endif
            success = false;
        }
    }

    // Initialize standard files
    String files[] = {"/config/settings.json", "/config/devices.json", "/config/mappings.json", "/config/schedules.json"};
    String defaultContent[] = {
        "{}",  // Empty JSON for settings
        "[]",  // Empty array for devices
        "{}",  // Empty JSON for mappings
        "[]"   // Empty array for schedules
    };

    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        if (!exists(files[i])) {
            #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
            #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
                Debug::println("[STORAGE][initializeStandardFiles] Creating standard file: " + files[i]);
            #endif
            #endif
            if (!write(files[i], defaultContent[i])) {
                #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
                #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
                    Debug::println("[STORAGE][initializeStandardFiles] Failed to create: " + files[i]);
                #endif
                #endif
                success = false;
            }
        }
    }

    return success;
}

void Storage::getFSInfo() {
    // No struct needed
    size_t total = LittleFS.totalBytes();
    size_t used = LittleFS.usedBytes();
    size_t free = LittleFS.totalBytes() - LittleFS.usedBytes();

    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][getFSInfo] Total: " + String(total));
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][getFSInfo] Used:  " + String(used));
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][getFSInfo] Free:  " + String(free));
    #endif
    #endif

    // Format callback string
    String info = "Total: " + String(total) + 
                 ", Used: " + String(used) + 
                 ", Free: " + String(free);

    triggerCallback("fs_info", info);
}

bool Storage::format() {
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][format] Formatting file system - all data will be lost!");
    #endif
    #endif

    if (LittleFS.format()) {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][format] File system formatted successfully");
        #endif
        #endif
        triggerCallback("formatted", "File system formatted");
        return initializeStandardFiles();
    } else {
        #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
            Debug::println("[STORAGE][format] Failed to format file system");
        #endif
        #endif
        triggerCallback("format_failed", "Failed to format file system");
        return false;
    }
}

void Storage::setCallback(StorageCallback callback) {
    _callback = callback;
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 3
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][StorageCallback] Storage callback set");
    #endif
    #endif
}

void Storage::triggerCallback(const String& event, const String& details) {
    if (_callback) {
        _callback(event, details);
    }

    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 3
        Debug::println("[STORAGE][triggerCallback] Event triggered: " + event + (details.length() > 0 ? " - " + details : ""));
    #endif
    #endif
}

#include "HardwareManager.h"
#include "EventBus.h" // 1. Include the new EventBus

// pin definitions are from /HardwareManager/Config.h
HardwareManager::HardwareManager()
    : _living(LC_CS, LC_SCK, LC_MISO, LC_MOSI, LC_GDO2),
      _rf(RF_RX, RF_TX),
      _kaku(),
      _ir(IR_RX, IR_TX)
{
}

void HardwareManager::init() {
#if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
    Debug::println("[HW] Initializing hardware components...");
#endif

    // Initialize storage first
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
    Debug::println("[HW] Init Storage Manager...");
    #endif
    if (_storage.init()) {
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[HW] Storage initialized successfully");
        #endif
        _storage.setCallback([this](const String& event, const String& details) {
            this->handleStorageEvent(event, details);
        });
    } else {
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
        Debug::println("[HW][ERROR] Storage initialization failed");
        #endif
    }

    _living.begin();
    _rf.init();

    // RF Callback -> Publish Event
    _rf.onCommand([this](const RFSignal& signal) {
        this->handleRFDecoded(signal);
        // Pass to Kaku decoder (Kaku is a specific protocol of RF)
        _kaku.onRawData(signal.value, signal.bits, signal.protocol, signal.pulse);
    });

    // Kaku Callback -> Publish Event (Optional: Kaku might emit its own specific events later)
    _kaku.onCommand([this](const RFCommand& cmd) {
        // We can create a specific "KAKU_EVENT" here if needed, 
        // but usually the raw RF signal + House/Btn logic in Translator is enough.
        // For now, we rely on the RF signal handler above.
        if (_kakuCb) _kakuCb(cmd); 
    });

    _ir.init();

    // IR Callback -> Publish Event
    _ir.onCommand([this](const IRCommand& cmd) {
        this->handleIRDecoded(cmd);
    });

    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
    Debug::println("[HW] HardwareManager initialization complete.");
    #endif
}

void HardwareManager::update() {
    _rf.update();
    _ir.update();
}

// --- Callback Setters (Kept for compatibility, but mostly unused now) ---
void HardwareManager::onRFCommand(RFCallback callback) { _rfCb = callback; }
void HardwareManager::onIRCommand(IRCallback callback) { _irCb = callback; }
void HardwareManager::onKakuCommand(KakuCallback callback) { _kakuCb = callback; }
void HardwareManager::onStorageEvent(Storage::StorageCallback callback) {
    _storage.setCallback([this, callback](const String& event, const String& details) {
        callback(event, details);
    });
}

void HardwareManager::handleRFDecoded(const RFSignal& signal) {
    Debug::println("[HW][DEBUG] Received RF signal: 0x" + String(signal.value, HEX));

    // 1. Create the Event using RAW data (House/Button unknown here)
    SystemEvent evt;
    evt.source = "RF";
    // Use Raw Value as identifier. 
    // Example: "RF_RAW_123456"
    evt.identifier = "RF_RAW_" + String(signal.value, HEX); 
    evt.rawData = String(signal.value, HEX);

    // 2. Publish to Core/Translator
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
            Debug::println("[HW][handleRFDecoder] Publishing RF event to EventBus...");
        #endif
    #endif
    EventBus::getInstance().publish(evt);

    // 3. IMPORTANT: Still pass raw data to KakuDecoder so it can decode House/Button
    // The KakuDecoder will then trigger its OWN callback/event with the detailed info.
    _kaku.onRawData(signal.value, signal.bits, signal.protocol, signal.pulse);
}   

void HardwareManager::handleIRDecoded(const IRCommand& cmd) {
    Debug::println("[HW][DEBUG] Received IR code: 0x" + String(cmd.code, HEX));

    // 1. Create the Event
    SystemEvent evt;
    evt.source = "IR";
    evt.identifier = "IR_RAW_"  + String(cmd.code, HEX); // Translator will look this up
    evt.rawData = "0x" + String(cmd.code, HEX);

    // 2. Publish to Core/Translator
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 1
            Debug::println("[HW][handleIRDecoded] Publishing IR event to EventBus...");
        #endif
    #endif

    EventBus::getInstance().publish(evt);

    // 3. (Optional) Keep old callback
    // if (_irCb) _irCb(cmd);
}

void HardwareManager::handleStorageEvent(const String& event, const String& details) {
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[HW][INFO] Storage event: " + event);
    #endif
    
    // Publish storage events too (e.g., "CONFIG_SAVED")
    SystemEvent evt;
    evt.source = "STORAGE";
    evt.identifier = event;
    evt.rawData = details;
    #ifdef DEBUG_LEVEL
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
            Debug::println("[HW][handleStorageEvent] Publishing storage event to EventBus...");
        #endif  
    #endif
    
    EventBus::getInstance().publish(evt); // Publish to Core/Translator 
}

#include "Translator.h"


std::vector<IrButtonMap> Translator::_irMap;

void Translator::init() {
    Debug::println("[TRANSLATOR] Initializing...");
    loadIrDatabase();
    
    // Subscribe to EventBus to listen for raw IR/RF codes
    EventBus::getInstance().subscribe(handleEvent);
}

void Translator::loadIrDatabase() {
    Debug::println("[TRANSLATOR] Loading IR databases from /ir_db/...");
    
    // Open the directory
    File root = LittleFS.open("/ir_db");
    if (!root || !root.isDirectory()) {
        Debug::println("[TRANSLATOR][ERROR] /ir_db/ directory not found!");
        return;
    }

    File file = root.openNextFile();
    while (file) {
        if (!file.isDirectory() && String(file.name()).endsWith(".json")) {
            Debug::println("[TRANSLATOR] Loading: " + String(file.name()));
            
            // Parse JSON
            DynamicJsonDocument doc(4096);
            DeserializationError error = deserializeJson(doc, file);
            
            if (error) {
                Debug::println("[TRANSLATOR][ERROR] Failed to parse " + String(file.name()));
            } else {
                String remoteId = doc["id"] | "UNKNOWN";
                JsonArray buttons = doc["buttons"];
                
                for (JsonObject btn : buttons) {
                    IrButtonMap map;
                    map.remoteId = remoteId;
                    map.buttonName = btn["name"] | "";
                    
                    // Get code string
                    String rawCode = btn["code"] | "0x00000000";
                    
                    // FIX: Sanitize string to remove hidden null-terminators
                    // c_str() stops at the first \0, rebuilding a clean String
                    map.code = String(rawCode.c_str()); 
                    map.code.trim(); // Remove any whitespace
                    map.code.toLowerCase(); // Force lowercase immediately

                    map.virtualColor = btn["virtual_color"] | "";
                    map.virtualColor.trim();

                    if (map.virtualColor != "") {
                        _irMap.push_back(map);
                        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 3
                        Debug::println("  Mapped: " + map.code + " -> " + map.virtualColor + " (" + map.remoteId + ")");
                        #endif
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        file = root.openNextFile();
    }
    
    Debug::println("[TRANSLATOR] Loaded " + String(_irMap.size()) + " IR button mappings.");
    root.close();
}

String Translator::resolveIrCode(const String& rawCode, const String& sourceRemote) {
    // Normalize input code
    String searchCode = rawCode;
    if (!searchCode.startsWith("0x")) {
        searchCode = "0x" + searchCode;
    }
    searchCode.trim();
    searchCode.toLowerCase();

    for (const auto& map : _irMap) {
        // map.code is already clean and lowercase from loadIrDatabase
        if (map.code == searchCode) {
            if (sourceRemote == "" || map.remoteId == sourceRemote) {
                return map.virtualColor;
            }
        }
    }
    return ""; // No match found
}

void Translator::handleEvent(const SystemEvent& evt) {
    // Only process IR events for now
    if (evt.source == "IR") {
        // evt.identifier is like "IR_RAW_fb04ef00"
        String rawCode = evt.identifier.substring(7); // Remove "IR_RAW_"
        
        String virtualColor = resolveIrCode(rawCode);
        
        if (virtualColor != "") {
            Debug::println("[TRANSLATOR] Translated " + evt.identifier + " -> " + virtualColor);
            
            // Publish the new Virtual Color event
            SystemEvent newEvt;
            newEvt.source = "VIRTUAL_COLOR";
            newEvt.identifier = virtualColor;
            newEvt.rawData = evt.rawData;
            EventBus::getInstance().publish(newEvt);
        } else {
            Debug::println("[TRANSLATOR] Unknown IR Code: " + rawCode);
        }
    }
}

void Translator::update() {
    // Nothing needed here yet, everything is event-driven
}

#include "ScriptManager.h"

ScriptManager::ScriptManager(HardwareManager& hw)
    : hardware(hw) {}

void ScriptManager::begin() {
    if (!LittleFS.begin()) {
        Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER][ERROR] LittleFS mount failed!");
        return;
    }

    loadScripts();
    EventBus::getInstance().subscribe([this](const SystemEvent& evt) {
        handleSystemEvent(evt);
    });
    Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER] Event trigger listener installed");
}

bool ScriptManager::loadScripts() {
    File file = LittleFS.open(SCRIPT_FILE, "r");
    if (!file) {
        scriptCount = 0;
        Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER] No scripts file found; starting empty");
        return true;
    }

    DynamicJsonDocument doc(JSON_DOC_SIZE);
    DeserializationError err = deserializeJson(doc, file);
    file.close();
    if (err) {
        Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER][ERROR] JSON parse failed: " + String(err.c_str()));
        return false;
    }

    JsonArray arr = doc.as<JsonArray>();
    scriptCount = 0;
    for (JsonObject obj : arr) {
        if (scriptCount >= MAX_SCRIPTS) break;
        Script& s = scripts[scriptCount++];
        s.id = obj["id"] | 0;
        s.name = obj["name"] | "";
        s.alias = obj["alias"] | "";

        if (obj["aliases"].is<JsonArray>()) {
            s.aliases = jsonArrayToString(obj["aliases"].as<JsonArray>());
        } else {
            DynamicJsonDocument aDoc(512);
            JsonArray a = aDoc.to<JsonArray>();
            if (s.alias.length()) a.add(s.alias);
            serializeJson(a, s.aliases);
        }

        if (obj["triggers"].is<JsonArray>()) {
            serializeJson(obj["triggers"], s.triggers);
        } else {
            s.triggers = "[]";
        }
        s.actions = obj["actions"].as<String>();
        if (s.actions.length() == 0) s.actions = "[]";
    }

    Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER] Loaded " + String(scriptCount) + " action(s)");
    return true;
}

bool ScriptManager::saveScripts() {
    DynamicJsonDocument doc(JSON_DOC_SIZE);
    JsonArray arr = doc.to<JsonArray>();

    for (int i = 0; i < scriptCount; ++i) {
        const Script& s = scripts[i];
        JsonObject obj = arr.createNestedObject();
        obj["id"] = s.id;
        obj["name"] = s.name;
        obj["alias"] = s.alias;

        DynamicJsonDocument aDoc(1024);
        JsonArray aliases = aDoc.to<JsonArray>();
        if (s.aliases.length()) {
            if (deserializeJson(aDoc, s.aliases)) {
                aliases = aDoc.to<JsonArray>();
            }
        }
        if (aliases.isNull() || aliases.size() == 0) {
            if (s.alias.length()) aliases.add(s.alias);
        }
        obj["aliases"] = aliases;

        DynamicJsonDocument tDoc(4096);
        JsonArray triggers = tDoc.to<JsonArray>();
        if (s.triggers.length()) {
            if (deserializeJson(tDoc, s.triggers)) {
                triggers = tDoc.to<JsonArray>();
            }
        }
        obj["triggers"] = triggers;

        DynamicJsonDocument actionsDoc(8192);
        if (deserializeJson(actionsDoc, s.actions)) {
            obj["actions"] = serialized(s.actions);
        } else {
            obj["actions"] = actionsDoc.as<JsonArray>();
        }
    }

    File file = LittleFS.open(SCRIPT_FILE, "w");
    if (!file) {
        Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER][ERROR] Cannot open scripts file for writing");
        return false;
    }
    serializeJson(arr, file);
    file.close();
    Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER] Scripts saved");
    return true;
}

bool ScriptManager::parseScriptObject(const String& json, JsonObject& out, DynamicJsonDocument& doc) {
    DeserializationError err = deserializeJson(doc, json);
    if (err || !doc.is<JsonObject>()) {
        Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER][ERROR] Invalid script JSON: " + String(err ? err.c_str() : "not an object"));
        return false;
    }
    out = doc.as<JsonObject>();
    return true;
}

bool ScriptManager::addScript(const String& json) {
    if (scriptCount >= MAX_SCRIPTS) return false;
    DynamicJsonDocument doc(JSON_DOC_SIZE);
    JsonObject obj;
    if (!parseScriptObject(json, obj, doc)) return false;

    Script& s = scripts[scriptCount++];
    s.id = nextScriptId();
    s.name = obj["name"] | "Unnamed action";
    s.alias = obj["alias"] | "";

    if (obj["aliases"].is<JsonArray>()) serializeJson(obj["aliases"], s.aliases);
    else {
        DynamicJsonDocument aDoc(512); JsonArray a = aDoc.to<JsonArray>();
        if (s.alias.length()) a.add(s.alias);
        serializeJson(a, s.aliases);
    }

    if (obj["triggers"].is<JsonArray>()) serializeJson(obj["triggers"], s.triggers);
    else s.triggers = "[]";
    if (obj["actions"].is<JsonArray>()) serializeJson(obj["actions"], s.actions);
    else s.actions = "[]";

    return saveScripts();
}

bool ScriptManager::updateScript(int scriptId, const String& json) {
    int idx = findScriptIndex(scriptId);
    if (idx < 0) return false;
    DynamicJsonDocument doc(JSON_DOC_SIZE);
    JsonObject obj;
    if (!parseScriptObject(json, obj, doc)) return false;

    Script& s = scripts[idx];
    s.name = obj["name"] | s.name;
    s.alias = obj["alias"] | "";
    if (obj["aliases"].is<JsonArray>()) serializeJson(obj["aliases"], s.aliases);
    else {
        DynamicJsonDocument aDoc(512); JsonArray a = aDoc.to<JsonArray>();
        if (s.alias.length()) a.add(s.alias);
        serializeJson(a, s.aliases);
    }
    if (obj["triggers"].is<JsonArray>()) serializeJson(obj["triggers"], s.triggers);
    if (obj["actions"].is<JsonArray>()) serializeJson(obj["actions"], s.actions);
    return saveScripts();
}

bool ScriptManager::deleteScript(int scriptId) {
    int idx = findScriptIndex(scriptId);
    if (idx < 0) return false;
    for (int i = idx; i < scriptCount - 1; ++i) scripts[i] = scripts[i + 1];
    --scriptCount;
    return saveScripts();
}

bool ScriptManager::executeScript(int scriptId) {
    int idx = findScriptIndex(scriptId);
    if (idx < 0) return false;

    DynamicJsonDocument doc(JSON_DOC_SIZE);
    DeserializationError err = deserializeJson(doc, scripts[idx].actions);
    if (err || !doc.is<JsonArray>()) {
        Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER][ERROR] Invalid actions JSON for " + scripts[idx].name);
        return false;
    }

    Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER] Executing: " + scripts[idx].name);
    bool ok = true;
    for (JsonObject action : doc.as<JsonArray>()) {
        if (!executeActionObject(action)) ok = false;
    }
    return ok;
}

bool ScriptManager::executeAction(const String& actionJson) {
    DynamicJsonDocument doc(4096);
    DeserializationError err = deserializeJson(doc, actionJson);
    if (err || !doc.is<JsonObject>()) return false;
    return executeActionObject(doc.as<JsonObject>());
}

bool ScriptManager::executeActionObject(JsonObject action) {
    String type = action["type"] | "";

    if (type == "living") {
        String deviceId = action["deviceId"] | "";
        String color = action["color"] | "#ffffff";
        int intensity = constrain((int)(action["intensity"] | 100), 0, 100);
        uint8_t r, g, b;
        parseHexColor(color, r, g, b);
        r = (uint8_t)((uint16_t)r * intensity / 100);
        g = (uint8_t)((uint16_t)g * intensity / 100);
        b = (uint8_t)((uint16_t)b * intensity / 100);

        if (deviceId == "all" || deviceId == "0-11") {
            hardware.living().setColorRGBAll(r, g, b);
            return true;
        }

        int dash = deviceId.indexOf('-');
        if (dash >= 0) {
            int start = _max(0, deviceId.substring(0, dash).toInt());
            int end = _min(LAMP_COUNT - 1, deviceId.substring(dash + 1).toInt());
            if (start > end) return false;
            for (int i = start; i <= end; ++i) hardware.living().setColorRGB(i, r, g, b);
            return true;
        }

        int lamp = deviceId.toInt();
        if (lamp < 0 || lamp >= LAMP_COUNT) return false;
        hardware.living().setColorRGB(lamp, r, g, b);
        return true;
    }

    if (type == "ir") {
        IRCommand cmd{};
        String codeText = action["code"] | "0";
        cmd.code = parseUnsigned(codeText);
        cmd.bits = action["bits"] | 32;
        cmd.protocol = action["protocol"] | 0;
        cmd.timestamp = millis();
        cmd.rawCodeLength = 0;
        cmd.hasRaw = false;
        if (cmd.code == 0) return false;
        hardware.ir().send(cmd);
        return true;
    }

    if (type == "rf") {
        String codeText = action["code"] | "0";
        unsigned long value = parseUnsigned(codeText);
        int bits = action["bits"] | 24;
        int protocol = action["protocol"] | 1;
        int pulse = action["pulse"] | 350;
        if (value == 0) return false;

        Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER][RF] Sending 0x" + String(value, HEX) +
                       " bits=" + String(bits) + " protocol=" + String(protocol) +
                       " pulse=" + String(pulse));
        hardware.rf().disableReceive();
        hardware.rf().setProtocol(protocol);
        hardware.rf().setPulseLength(pulse);
        hardware.rf().send(value, bits);
        hardware.rf().enableReceive();
        return true;
    }

    Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER][ERROR] Unknown action type: " + type);
    return false;
}

String ScriptManager::getScriptsAsJson() {
    DynamicJsonDocument doc(JSON_DOC_SIZE);
    JsonArray arr = doc.to<JsonArray>();
    for (int i = 0; i < scriptCount; ++i) {
        const Script& s = scripts[i];
        JsonObject obj = arr.createNestedObject();
        obj["id"] = s.id;
        obj["name"] = s.name;
        obj["alias"] = s.alias;

        DynamicJsonDocument aDoc(1024);
        if (!s.aliases.length() || deserializeJson(aDoc, s.aliases)) {
            JsonArray a = aDoc.to<JsonArray>();
            if (s.alias.length()) a.add(s.alias);
        }
        obj["aliases"] = aDoc.as<JsonArray>();

        DynamicJsonDocument tDoc(4096);
        if (!s.triggers.length() || deserializeJson(tDoc, s.triggers)) tDoc.to<JsonArray>();
        obj["triggers"] = tDoc.as<JsonArray>();

        DynamicJsonDocument actDoc(8192);
        if (!s.actions.length() || deserializeJson(actDoc, s.actions)) actDoc.to<JsonArray>();
        obj["actions"] = actDoc.as<JsonArray>();
    }
    String out; serializeJson(arr, out); return out;
}

String ScriptManager::getScriptById(int scriptId) {
    int idx = findScriptIndex(scriptId);
    if (idx < 0) return "{}";
    DynamicJsonDocument all(JSON_DOC_SIZE);
    deserializeJson(all, getScriptsAsJson());
    for (JsonObject obj : all.as<JsonArray>()) if ((int)(obj["id"] | 0) == scriptId) {
        String out; serializeJson(obj, out); return out;
    }
    return "{}";
}

void ScriptManager::startCapture(const String& filter) {
    captureFilter = filter; captureFilter.toUpperCase();
    captureWaiting = true;
    capturedSource = capturedIdentifier = capturedRawData = "";
    captureTime = millis();
    Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER][LEARN] Waiting for " + captureFilter + " event...");
}

String ScriptManager::getCaptureStatus() const {
    DynamicJsonDocument doc(1024);
    doc["waiting"] = captureWaiting;
    doc["filter"] = captureFilter;
    doc["source"] = capturedSource;
    doc["identifier"] = capturedIdentifier;
    doc["rawData"] = capturedRawData;
    doc["ageMs"] = captureTime ? (millis() - captureTime) : 0;
    String out; serializeJson(doc, out); return out;
}

bool ScriptManager::eventMatchesCapture(const SystemEvent& evt) const {
    if (captureFilter == "ANY") return true;
    return evt.source.equalsIgnoreCase(captureFilter);
}

bool ScriptManager::matchesTrigger(JsonArray triggers, const SystemEvent& evt) const {
    for (JsonObject trigger : triggers) {
        String source = trigger["source"] | "";
        String identifier = trigger["identifier"] | "";
        if (source.equalsIgnoreCase(evt.source) && identifier.equalsIgnoreCase(evt.identifier)) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

void ScriptManager::handleSystemEvent(const SystemEvent& evt) {
    if (captureWaiting && eventMatchesCapture(evt)) {
        capturedSource = evt.source;
        capturedIdentifier = evt.identifier;
        capturedRawData = evt.rawData;
        captureWaiting = false;
        Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER][LEARN] Captured " + evt.source + " -> " + evt.identifier);
        return;
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < scriptCount; ++i) {
        DynamicJsonDocument tDoc(4096);
        if (deserializeJson(tDoc, scripts[i].triggers)) continue;
        JsonArray triggers = tDoc.as<JsonArray>();
        if (matchesTrigger(triggers, evt)) {
            Debug::println("[SCRIPTMANAGER][TRIGGER] " + evt.source + "/" + evt.identifier +
                           " -> " + scripts[i].name);
            executeScript(scripts[i].id);
        }
    }
}

uint32_t ScriptManager::parseUnsigned(const String& text) {
    String s = text; s.trim();
    if (s.startsWith("0x") || s.startsWith("0X")) s = s.substring(2);
    return (uint32_t)strtoul(s.c_str(), nullptr, 16);
}

void ScriptManager::parseHexColor(const String& color, uint8_t& r, uint8_t& g, uint8_t& b) {
    String s = color; s.trim();
    if (s.length() == 7 && s[0] == '#') {
        r = (uint8_t)strtoul(s.substring(1, 3).c_str(), nullptr, 16);
        g = (uint8_t)strtoul(s.substring(3, 5).c_str(), nullptr, 16);
        b = (uint8_t)strtoul(s.substring(5, 7).c_str(), nullptr, 16);
    } else r = g = b = 255;
}

String ScriptManager::jsonArrayToString(JsonArray array) {
    String out; serializeJson(array, out); return out;
}

int ScriptManager::findScriptIndex(int scriptId) {
    for (int i = 0; i < scriptCount; ++i) if (scripts[i].id == scriptId) return i;
    return -1;
}

int ScriptManager::nextScriptId() const {
    int maxId = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < scriptCount; ++i) if (scripts[i].id > maxId) maxId = scripts[i].id;
    return maxId + 1;
}

#pragma once
#include <Arduino.h>
#include <LittleFS.h>
#include <ArduinoJson.h>
#include "../Debug/Debug.h"
#include "../Core/EventBus.h"
#include "../HardwareManager/HardwareManager.h"

/**
 * Action/scene manager.
 *
 * IMPORTANT: this class deliberately uses the existing HardwareManager API;
 * HardwareManager and Debug are not modified by this package.
 */
class ScriptManager {
public:
    explicit ScriptManager(HardwareManager& hardware);

    void begin();
    bool loadScripts();
    bool saveScripts();
    bool addScript(const String& script);
    bool updateScript(int scriptId, const String& script);
    bool deleteScript(int scriptId);
    bool executeScript(int scriptId);
    bool executeAction(const String& actionJson);

    String getScriptsAsJson();
    String getScriptById(int scriptId);

    // Learn the next incoming event. filter: ANY, KAKU, IR or RF.
    void startCapture(const String& filter = "ANY");
    String getCaptureStatus() const;

private:
    static const int MAX_SCRIPTS = 50;
    static const size_t JSON_DOC_SIZE = 16384;

    struct Script {
        int id = 0;
        String name;
        String alias;       // backwards-compatible first alias
        String aliases;     // JSON array string
        String triggers;    // JSON array string
        String actions;     // JSON array string
    };

    HardwareManager& hardware;
    Script scripts[MAX_SCRIPTS];
    int scriptCount = 0;
    const char* SCRIPT_FILE = "/scripts.json";

    volatile bool captureWaiting = false;
    String captureFilter = "ANY";
    String capturedSource;
    String capturedIdentifier;
    String capturedRawData;
    unsigned long captureTime = 0;

    int findScriptIndex(int scriptId);
    int nextScriptId() const;

    bool parseScriptObject(const String& json, JsonObject& out, DynamicJsonDocument& doc);
    bool executeActionObject(JsonObject action);
    bool matchesTrigger(JsonArray triggers, const SystemEvent& evt) const;
    bool eventMatchesCapture(const SystemEvent& evt) const;
    void handleSystemEvent(const SystemEvent& evt);

    static uint32_t parseUnsigned(const String& text);
    static void parseHexColor(const String& color, uint8_t& r, uint8_t& g, uint8_t& b);
    static String jsonArrayToString(JsonArray array);
};

#include "WebUI.h"
#include <LittleFS.h>
#include <ArduinoJson.h>

WebUI::WebUI(HardwareManager& hw)
    : server(80), scriptManager(hw) {}

void WebUI::begin() {
    beginAP();
    scriptManager.begin();
}

void WebUI::beginAP() {
    Debug::println("[WEBUI] Initializing WebUI in Access Point mode...");
    WiFi.mode(WIFI_AP);
    IPAddress localIP(192,168,4,1), gateway(192,168,4,1), subnet(255,255,255,0);
    WiFi.softAPConfig(localIP, gateway, subnet);
    if (!WiFi.softAP("HomeController", "password123")) {
        Debug::println("[WEBUI][ERROR] Failed to start Access Point!");
        return;
    }
    Debug::println("[WEBUI] Access Point started with IP: " + WiFi.softAPIP().toString());
    if (!LittleFS.begin()) {
        Debug::println("[WEBUI][ERROR] LittleFS mount failed!");
        return;
    }
    setupRoutes();
    server.begin();
    Debug::println("[WEBUI] Web server started on port 80");
    delay(100);
}

static bool getPostBody(AsyncWebServerRequest* request, String& body) {
    if (!request->hasParam("body", true)) return false;
    body = request->getParam("body", true)->value();
    return body.length() > 0;
}

void WebUI::setupRoutes() {
    server.on("/", HTTP_GET, [](AsyncWebServerRequest* request) {
        request->send(LittleFS, "/index.html", "text/html");
    });

    server.on("/api/logs", HTTP_GET, [](AsyncWebServerRequest* request) {
        request->send(200, "text/html", Debug::getWebLogs());
    });

    server.on("/api/action-scripts", HTTP_GET, [this](AsyncWebServerRequest* request) {
        request->send(200, "application/json", scriptManager.getScriptsAsJson());
    });

    server.on("/api/action-scripts", HTTP_POST, [this](AsyncWebServerRequest* request) {
        String body;
        if (!getPostBody(request, body)) {
            request->send(400, "application/json", "{\"success\":false,\"message\":\"Send form field 'body' containing JSON\"}");
            return;
        }
        DynamicJsonDocument doc(16384);
        if (deserializeJson(doc, body) || !doc.is<JsonObject>()) {
            request->send(400, "application/json", "{\"success\":false,\"message\":\"Invalid JSON\"}");
            return;
        }
        int id = doc["id"] | 0;
        bool ok = id > 0 ? scriptManager.updateScript(id, body) : scriptManager.addScript(body);
        request->send(ok ? 200 : 500, "application/json",
                      ok ? "{\"success\":true}" : "{\"success\":false,\"message\":\"Save failed\"}");
    });

    server.on("/api/action-scripts", HTTP_DELETE, [this](AsyncWebServerRequest* request) {
        if (!request->hasParam("id")) {
            request->send(400, "application/json", "{\"success\":false,\"message\":\"Missing id\"}");
            return;
        }
        bool ok = scriptManager.deleteScript(request->getParam("id")->value().toInt());
        request->send(ok ? 200 : 404, "application/json", ok ? "{\"success\":true}" : "{\"success\":false}");
    });

    server.on("/api/execute-script", HTTP_POST, [this](AsyncWebServerRequest* request) {
        String body;
        if (!getPostBody(request, body)) {
            request->send(400, "application/json", "{\"success\":false,\"message\":\"Missing body\"}");
            return;
        }
        DynamicJsonDocument doc(4096);
        if (deserializeJson(doc, body)) {
            request->send(400, "application/json", "{\"success\":false,\"message\":\"Invalid JSON\"}");
            return;
        }
        int id = doc["id"] | 0;
        bool ok = id > 0 && scriptManager.executeScript(id);
        request->send(ok ? 200 : 500, "application/json", ok ? "{\"success\":true}" : "{\"success\":false}");
    });

    server.on("/api/test-action", HTTP_POST, [this](AsyncWebServerRequest* request) {
        String body;
        if (!getPostBody(request, body)) {
            request->send(400, "application/json", "{\"success\":false,\"message\":\"Missing action body\"}");
            return;
        }
        bool ok = scriptManager.executeAction(body);
        request->send(ok ? 200 : 500, "application/json", ok ? "{\"success\":true}" : "{\"success\":false}");
    });

    server.on("/api/learn", HTTP_POST, [this](AsyncWebServerRequest* request) {
        String filter = request->hasParam("filter", true) ? request->getParam("filter", true)->value() : "ANY";
        scriptManager.startCapture(filter);
        request->send(200, "application/json", "{\"success\":true}");
    });

    server.on("/api/learn", HTTP_GET, [this](AsyncWebServerRequest* request) {
        request->send(200, "application/json", scriptManager.getCaptureStatus());
    });

    server.on("/action-scripts.html", HTTP_GET, [](AsyncWebServerRequest* request) {
        request->send(LittleFS, "/action_scripts.html", "text/html");
    });

    Debug::println("[WEBUI] Web routes configured");
}

void WebUI::loop() {}

    <!-- Status Bar -->

        <span>Connection:</span>
        <div id="connection-led" class="led"></div>

    <h1>Action Script Editor</h1>

    <!-- Action Script Form -->
    <div class="editor-container">
        <h2>Create/Edit Action Script</h2>
        <form id="script-form" class="script-form">
            <div class="form-group">
                <label for="script-name">Script Name:</label>
                <input type="text" id="script-name" placeholder="e.g., Red Scene" required>
            </div>

            <div class="form-group">
                <label for="script-alias">Alias (optional):</label>
                <input type="text" id="script-alias" placeholder="e.g., All Red">
            </div>

            <div class="form-group">
                <label for="device-type">Device Type:</label>
                <select id="device-type">
                    <option value="living">Living Colors (LED Strips)</option>
                    <option value="rf">RF Switch</option>
                    <option value="kaku">Kaku Switch</option>
                </select>
            </div>

            <div class="form-group">
                <label for="device-id">Device ID:</label>
                <input type="text" id="device-id" placeholder="e.g., 0-11 for all lamps">
            </div>

            <div class="form-group">
                <label for="color">Color:</label>
                <input type="color" id="color" value="#ff0000">
                <div id="color-preview" class="color-preview" style="background-color: #ff0000;"></div>
            </div>

            <div class="form-group">
                <label for="intensity">Intensity (0-100):</label>
                <input type="range" id="intensity" min="0" max="100" value="100">
                <span id="intensity-value">100%</span>
            </div>
        </form>

        <div style="margin-top: 20px;">
            <button class="btn" id="add-action">Add Action</button>
            <button class="btn btn-success" id="save-script">Save Script</button>
            <button class="btn" id="preview-script">Preview Script</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Current Actions List -->
    <div class="action-list">
        <h2>Current Actions</h2>
        <div id="actions-container">
            <!-- Actions will be dynamically added here -->
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Saved Scripts List -->
    <div class="action-list">
        <h2>Saved Scripts</h2>
        <div id="scripts-container">
            <!-- Scripts will be dynamically loaded here -->
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        // Global variables
        let currentActions = [];
        let currentScriptId = null;
        let connectionLed = document.getElementById('connection-led');
        let connectionStatus = document.getElementById('connection-status');

        // Connection status
        window.onload = function() {
            updateConnectionStatus(true);
            loadScripts();

            // Update color preview when color changes
            document.getElementById('color').addEventListener('input', function(e) {
                document.getElementById('color-preview').style.backgroundColor = e.target.value;
            });

            // Update intensity value
            document.getElementById('intensity').addEventListener('input', function(e) {
                document.getElementById('intensity-value').textContent = e.target.value + '%';
            });

            // Add action button
            document.getElementById('add-action').addEventListener('click', addAction);

            // Save script button
            document.getElementById('save-script').addEventListener('click', saveScript);

            // Preview script button
            document.getElementById('preview-script').addEventListener('click', previewScript);
        };

        function updateConnectionStatus(connected) {
            if (connected) {
                connectionLed.classList.add('on');
                connectionStatus.textContent = 'Connected';
                connectionStatus.style.color = '#0f0';
            } else {
                connectionLed.classList.remove('on');
                connectionStatus.textContent = 'Disconnected';
                connectionStatus.style.color = '#f00';
            }
        }

        function addAction() {
            const deviceType = document.getElementById('device-type').value;
            const deviceId = document.getElementById('device-id').value;
            const color = document.getElementById('color').value;
            const intensity = document.getElementById('intensity').value;

            // Validate inputs
            if (!deviceId) {
                alert('Please enter a device ID');
                return;
            }

            // Add action to current list
            const action = {
                id: Date.now(), // Simple unique ID
                type: deviceType,
                deviceId: deviceId,
                color: color,
                intensity: intensity
            };

            currentActions.push(action);
            renderActions();

            // Reset form
            document.getElementById('device-id').value = '';
        }

        function removeAction(actionId) {
            currentActions = currentActions.filter(action => action.id !== actionId);
            renderActions();
        }

        function renderActions() {
            const container = document.getElementById('actions-container');
            container.innerHTML = '';

            if (currentActions.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<p>No actions added yet.</p>';
                return;
            }

            currentActions.forEach(action => {
                const actionDiv = document.createElement('div');
                actionDiv.className = 'action-item';

                const actionDetails = document.createElement('div');
                actionDetails.className = 'action-details';
                actionDetails.innerHTML = `
                    <strong>${action.type.toUpperCase()}:</strong> Device ${action.deviceId}<br>
                    Color: ${action.color} | Intensity: ${action.intensity}%
                `;

                const removeBtn = document.createElement('button');
                removeBtn.className = 'btn btn-danger';
                removeBtn.textContent = 'Remove';
                removeBtn.onclick = () => removeAction(action.id);

                actionDiv.appendChild(actionDetails);
                actionDiv.appendChild(removeBtn);
                container.appendChild(actionDiv);
            });
        }

        function saveScript() {
            const name = document.getElementById('script-name').value;
            const alias = document.getElementById('script-alias').value;

            if (!name) {
                alert('Please enter a script name');
                return;
            }

            if (currentActions.length === 0) {
                alert('Please add at least one action');
                return;
            }

            const script = {
                id: currentScriptId || Date.now(),
                name: name,
                alias: alias,
                actions: currentActions
            };

            // Save to server
            fetch('/api/action-scripts', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
                body: JSON.stringify(script)
            })
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.success) {
                    alert('Script saved successfully!');
                    currentScriptId = null;
                    currentActions = [];
                    renderActions();
                    loadScripts();
                } else {
                    alert('Error saving script: ' + data.message);
                }
            })
            .catch(error => {
                alert('Error: ' + error);
            });
        }

        function previewScript() {
            if (currentActions.length === 0) {
                alert('Please add at least one action');
                return;
            }

            // Create a temporary script with current actions
            const tempScript = {
                id: Date.now(), // Temporary ID
                name: "Preview",
                alias: "",
                actions: currentActions
            };

            // Send preview request to server
            fetch('/api/execute-script', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
                body: JSON.stringify(tempScript)
            })
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.success) {
                    alert('Preview executed! Check your devices.');
                } else {
                    alert('Error executing preview: ' + data.message);
                }
            })
            .catch(error => {
                alert('Error: ' + error);
            });
        }

        function loadScripts() {
            fetch('/api/action-scripts')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                const container = document.getElementById('scripts-container');
                container.innerHTML = '';

                if (data.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = '<p>No saved scripts yet.</p>';
                    return;
                }

                data.forEach(script => {
                    const scriptDiv = document.createElement('div');
                    scriptDiv.className = 'action-item';

                    const scriptDetails = document.createElement('div');
                    scriptDetails.className = 'action-details';
                    scriptDetails.innerHTML = `
                        <strong>${script.name}</strong>
                        ${script.alias ? `<br><small>Alias: ${script.alias}</small>` : ''}
                        <br><small>${script.actions.length} action(s)</small>
                    `;

                    const actionsBtn = document.createElement('button');
                    actionsBtn.className = 'btn';
                    actionsBtn.textContent = 'Edit';
                    actionsBtn.onclick = () => loadScript(script.id);

                    const deleteBtn = document.createElement('button');
                    deleteBtn.className = 'btn btn-danger';
                    deleteBtn.textContent = 'Delete';
                    deleteBtn.onclick = () => deleteScript(script.id);

                    const executeBtn = document.createElement('button');
                    executeBtn.className = 'btn btn-success';
                    executeBtn.textContent = 'Execute';
                    executeBtn.onclick = () => executeScript(script.id);

                    scriptDiv.appendChild(scriptDetails);
                    scriptDiv.appendChild(actionsBtn);
                    scriptDiv.appendChild(deleteBtn);
                    scriptDiv.appendChild(executeBtn);
                    container.appendChild(scriptDiv);
                });
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error loading scripts:', error);
            });
        }

        function loadScript(scriptId) {
            fetch(`/api/action-scripts`)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(scripts => {
                const script = scripts.find(s => s.id === scriptId);
                if (script) {
                    currentScriptId = script.id;
                    currentActions = script.actions;

                    document.getElementById('script-name').value = script.name;
                    document.getElementById('script-alias').value = script.alias || '';

                    renderActions();
                } else {
                    alert('Error loading script: Script not found');
                }
            })
            .catch(error => {
                alert('Error: ' + error);
            });
        }

        function deleteScript(scriptId) {
            if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this script?')) {
                return;
            }

            fetch('/api/action-scripts?id=' + scriptId, {
                method: 'DELETE'
            })
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.success) {
                    alert('Script deleted successfully!');
                    loadScripts();
                } else {
                    alert('Error deleting script: ' + data.message);
                }
            })
            .catch(error => {
                alert('Error: ' + error);
            });
        }

        function executeScript(scriptId) {
            fetch('/api/execute-script', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
                body: JSON.stringify({id: scriptId})
            })
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.success) {
                    alert('Script executed successfully!');
                } else {
                    alert('Error executing script: ' + data.message);
                }
            })
            .catch(error => {
                alert('Error: ' + error);
            });
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
</file>

<file path="data/index.html">
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>ESP32 Controller</title>
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
    <style>
        body { font-family: sans-serif; background: #222; color: #eee; margin: 0; padding: 10px; }
        h2 { margin-top: 0; }
        
        /* Heartbeat LED Styling */
        #status-bar {
            position: fixed; top: 10px; right: 10px;
            background: #333; padding: 10px; border-radius: 8px;
            display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
        }
        .led {
            width: 20px; height: 20px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background-color: #555; /* Off state */
            box-shadow: inset 0 2px 5px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
            transition: background-color 0.1s;
        }
        .led.on { background-color: #0f0; box-shadow: 0 0 10px #0f0; } /* Green On */
        .led.error { background-color: #f00; box-shadow: 0 0 10px #f00; } /* Red Error */

        /* Log Window Styling */
        #log-window {
            margin-top: 60px;
            background: #111; border: 1px solid #444;
            height: 80vh; overflow-y: scroll;
            padding: 10px; font-family: monospace; font-size: 14px;
            white-space: pre-wrap; word-wrap: break-word;
        }
        .log-line { margin: 2px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #222; }
        .highlight { color: #ffeb3b; font-weight: bold; } /* Highlight for BEAT */
        .error-log { color: #ff5555; }
    </style>
</head>
<body>

    <!-- Status Bar with Heartbeat LED -->
    <div id="status-bar">
        <span>System Heartbeat:</span>
        <div id="heartbeat-led" class="led"></div>
        <span id="connection-status" style="font-size: 0.8em; color: #aaa;">Connecting...</span>
    </div>

    <h2>Live Monitor</h2>
    <div style="margin-bottom: 15px;">
        <button class="btn" id="open-editor-btn">Open Action Script Editor</button>
    </div>
    <div id="log-window">Waiting for logs...</div>

    <script>
        let lastBeatTime = 0;
        let lastLogIndex = 0; // Simple tracker to avoid re-processing old logs
        const led = document.getElementById('heartbeat-led');
        const statusText = document.getElementById('connection-status');
        const logWindow = document.getElementById('log-window');

        // 1. Sync Time on Load
        window.onload = function() {
            const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
            fetch('/api/set-time', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
                body: JSON.stringify({ time: now }) 
            }).then(() => {
                statusText.innerText = "Time Synced";
                startPolling();
            }).catch(err => {
                statusText.innerText = "Time Sync Failed";
                startPolling(); // Start anyway
            });
        };

        // 2. Poll Logs every 500ms
        function startPolling() {
            setInterval(fetchLogs, 500);
        }

        function fetchLogs() {
            fetch('/api/logs') // We will create this route in C++
            .then(response => response.text())
            .then(text => {
                // Update Connection Status
                statusText.innerText = "Connected";
                statusText.style.color = "#0f0";
                lastBeatTime = Date.now(); // Received data, so connection is alive

                // Process Logs
                // Simple approach: replace entire content for now (robust for beginners)
                // For a real app, we'd append only new lines.
                logWindow.innerHTML = text; 
                logWindow.scrollTop = logWindow.scrollHeight; // Auto-scroll to bottom

                // Check for BEAT in the text
                if (text.includes("[PACKET] BEAT")) {
                    toggleLed();
                }

            })
            .catch(err => {
                statusText.innerText = "Disconnected";
                statusText.style.color = "#f00";
                // If no beat for 2 seconds, turn LED red
                if (Date.now() - lastBeatTime > 2000) {
                    led.className = 'led error';
                }
            });
        }

        function toggleLed() {
            // Toggle the 'on' class
            if (led.classList.contains('on')) {
                led.classList.remove('on');
            } else {
                led.classList.add('on');
            }
            lastBeatTime = Date.now();
        }

        // Safety: If we stop receiving beats but still have connection
        setInterval(() => {
            if (Date.now() - lastBeatTime > 2500) {
                led.className = 'led error'; // Turn Red
                statusText.innerText = "Heartbeat Lost";
            }
        }, 1000);

        // Handle "Open Action Script Editor" button click
        document.getElementById('open-editor-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            // Open the action script editor in a new tab
            window.open('/action-scripts.html', '_blank');
        });

    </script>
</body>
</html>
</file>

<file path="lib/Core/Core.cpp">
#include "Core.h"

Core::Core()
    : hardware(), webui()
{
    // Initialize test variables
    testRfWaiting = false;
    testRfEchoTime = 0;
    testRf = RFSignal();

    testIrWaiting = false;
    testIrEchoTime = 0;
    testIr = IRCommand();

    // Initialize echo variables
    _rfEchoTime = 0;
    _rfEchoCmd = RFSignal();

    _irEchoTime = 0;
    _irEchoCmd = IRCommand();
}

void Core::init() {
    pinMode(HEARTBEAT_PIN, OUTPUT);
    heartbeatState = LOW;
    lastHeartbeatTime = 0;

    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE][init] Starting Core::init()...");
    #endif

    // 1. Initialize Hardware (includes Storage)
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE][hardware.init] starts HardwareManager initialization...");
    #endif
    hardware.init();

    // 2. Initialise Translator
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE][Translator::init] starts Translator initialization...");
    #endif
    Translator::init();

    // 3. Initialize WebUI
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE] Initializing WebUI (Access Point mode)...");
    #endif
    webui.begin();

    // 4. Subscribe to EventBus (The NEW way)
    EventBus::getInstance().subscribe([this](const SystemEvent& evt) {
        this->handleSystemEvent(evt);
    });

    // 4. (Optional) Keep Legacy Callbacks for now if you still need them for debugging
    // If you want to fully test EventBus, comment these out so lights don't turn on twice!
    /*
    hardware.onRFCommand([this](const RFSignal& signal) { this->onRFCommand(signal); });
    hardware.onIRCommand([this](const IRCommand& cmd) { this->onIRCommand(cmd); });
    hardware.onKakuCommand([this](const RFCommand& cmd) { this->handleKaku(cmd); });
    */

    // 5. Storage Event Callback
    hardware.onStorageEvent([this](const String& event, const String& details) {
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
            Debug::println("[CORE][STORAGE] Event received: " + event + " - " + details);
        #endif
    });

    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE] Core initialization complete");
    #endif
}   


void Core::update() {
    
    hardware.update(); // Your existing hardware update

       // Heartbeat
    if (millis() - lastHeartbeatTime >= 500) {
        lastHeartbeatTime = millis();
        heartbeatState = !heartbeatState;
        digitalWrite(32, heartbeatState);
        
        if (heartbeatState == HIGH) {
            //Debug::packet("BEAT"); // This sends "[PACKET] BEAT" to the web log buffer
        }
    }
    /* // --- Heartbeat Logic ---
    if (millis() - lastHeartbeatTime >= 500) {
        lastHeartbeatTime = millis();
        heartbeatState = !heartbeatState; // Toggle
        digitalWrite(HEARTBEAT_PIN, heartbeatState);
        
        // Optional: Send a "beat" event to WebUI via WebSocket/Log
        if (heartbeatState == HIGH) {
             Debug::packet("BEAT"); // Use your packet logger for low overhead
        }
    }
*/
    // Update hardware
    hardware.update();

    // Update time
    unsigned long currentTime = millis();

    // ----------------------------------------------------
    // RF TEST ROUTINE (independent)
    // ----------------------------------------------------

    // Start RF timer when first RF code arrives
    if (!testRfWaiting && testRf.value) {
        testRfEchoTime = currentTime + 2000;
        testRfWaiting = true;
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE][TEST][RF] RF echo scheduled in 2 seconds");
        #endif
    }

    // Echo RF when timer expires
    if (testRfWaiting && currentTime >= testRfEchoTime) {
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE][TEST][RF] Echoing RF: 0x" + String(testRf.value, HEX));
        Debug::println("[CORE][TEST][RF] Protocol=" + String(testRf.protocol) +
                       " Bits=" + String(testRf.bits) +
                       " Pulse=" + String(testRf.pulse));
        #endif

        hardware.rf().disableReceive();
        hardware.rf().setProtocol(testRf.protocol);
        hardware.rf().setPulseLength(testRf.pulse);
        hardware.rf().send(testRf.value, testRf.bits);
        hardware.rf().enableReceive();

        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE][TEST][RF] RF echo complete");
        #endif

        // Reset RF test routine
        testRf = RFSignal();
        testRfWaiting = false;
        testRfEchoTime = 0;
    }

    // ----------------------------------------------------
    // IR TEST ROUTINE (independent)
    // ----------------------------------------------------

    // Start IR timer when first IR code arrives
    if (!testIrWaiting && testIr.code) {
        testIrEchoTime = currentTime + 2000;
        testIrWaiting = true;
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE][TEST][IR] IR echo scheduled in 2 seconds");
        #endif
    }

    // Echo IR when timer expires
    if (testIrWaiting && currentTime >= testIrEchoTime) {
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE][TEST][IR] Echoing IR: 0x" + String(testIr.code, HEX));
        #endif

        hardware.ir().send(testIr);

        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE][TEST][IR] IR echo complete");
        #endif

        // Reset IR test routine
        testIr = IRCommand();
        testIrWaiting = false;
        testIrEchoTime = 0;
    }

    // ----------------------------------------------------
    // REAL RF echo logic (your existing system)
    // ----------------------------------------------------
    if (_rfEchoTime && (long)(currentTime - _rfEchoTime) >= 0) {
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE][DEBUG][RF] Echoing RF code: 0x" + String(_rfEchoCmd.value, HEX));
        #endif

        if (_rfEchoCmd.value != 0) {
            hardware.rf().disableReceive();
            hardware.rf().setProtocol(_rfEchoCmd.protocol);
            hardware.rf().setPulseLength(_rfEchoCmd.pulse);
            hardware.rf().send(_rfEchoCmd.value, _rfEchoCmd.bits);
            hardware.rf().enableReceive();
        }

        _rfEchoTime = 0;
        _rfEchoCmd = RFSignal();
    }

    // ----------------------------------------------------
    // REAL IR echo logic (your existing system)
    // ----------------------------------------------------
    if (_irEchoTime && (long)(currentTime - _irEchoTime) >= 0) {
        #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
        Debug::println("[CORE][DEBUG][IR] Echoing IR code: 0x" + String(_irEchoCmd.code, HEX));
        #endif

        if (_irEchoCmd.code != 0) {
            hardware.ir().send(_irEchoCmd);
        }

        _irEchoTime = 0;
        _irEchoCmd = IRCommand();
    }
}

void Core::onRFCommand(const RFSignal& signal) {
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
    Debug::println("[CORE][TEST][RF] Received RF: 0x" + String(signal.value, HEX));

    if (!testRf.value) {
        testRf = signal;
        Debug::println("[CORE][TEST][RF] Stored first RF code");
    }
    #endif
}

void Core::onIRCommand(const IRCommand& cmd) {
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
    Debug::println("[CORE][TEST][IR] Received IR: 0x" + String(cmd.code, HEX));

    if (!testIr.code) {
        testIr = cmd;
        Debug::println("[CORE][TEST][IR] Stored first IR code");
    }
    #endif
}

void Core::handleKaku(const RFCommand& cmd) {
    #if DEBUG_LEVEL >= 2
    Debug::println("[KAKU] House " + String(cmd.house) +
                   " Button " + String(cmd.button));

    if (cmd.button == 2) {
        Debug::println("[CORE] Button 2 → BLUE");
    }
    #endif

    if (cmd.button == 2) {
        for (int i = 0; i < LAMP_COUNT; i++) {
            hardware.living().setColor(i, 160, 255, 255);
        }
    }
}


void Core::handleSystemEvent(const SystemEvent& evt) {
    Debug::println("[CORE][EVENT] Received: " + evt.source + " -> " + evt.identifier);

    if (evt.source == "KAKU" && evt.identifier == "KAKU_C_2") {
        Debug::println("[CORE][TEST] Kaku C-2 Detected via EventBus! Turning Lights Blue...");
        for (int i = 0; i < LAMP_COUNT; i++) {
            hardware.living().setColor(i, 160, 255, 255);
        }
    }

    // NEW: Handle Virtual Colors
    if (evt.source == "VIRTUAL_COLOR") {
        Debug::println("[CORE][COLOR] Received Virtual Color: " + evt.identifier);
        
        if (evt.identifier == "VC_RED") {
            Debug::println("[CORE][ACTION] Setting all lamps to RED!");
            // Convert RGB(255,0,0) to HSV
            uint8_t h, s, v;
            LivingColors::rgbToHsv(255, 0, 0, h, s, v);
            for (int i = 0; i < LAMP_COUNT; i++) {
                hardware.living().setColor(i, h, s, v);
            }
        } else if (evt.identifier == "VC_GREEN") {
            Debug::println("[CORE][ACTION] Setting all lamps to GREEN!");
            // Convert RGB(0,255,0) to HSV
            uint8_t h, s, v;
            LivingColors::rgbToHsv(0, 255, 0, h, s, v);
            for (int i = 0; i < LAMP_COUNT; i++) {
                hardware.living().setColor(i, h, s, v);
            }
        } else if (evt.identifier == "VC_BLUE") {
            Debug::println("[CORE][ACTION] Setting all lamps to BLUE!");
            // Convert RGB(0,0,255) to HSV
            uint8_t h, s, v;
            LivingColors::rgbToHsv(0, 0, 255, h, s, v);
            for (int i = 0; i < LAMP_COUNT; i++) {
                hardware.living().setColor(i, h, s, v);
            }
        }
    }
}
